                ah.Text = am >= 10 and 'Loaded' or 'Loading part ' .. tostring(am + 1) .. ' / 10'
            end)

                    end)

                            bf = nil
                        end

                        be = nil

                            bk()
                        end
                    end)

                end

                    local function n(cN)
                        if type(cN) == 'number' then

                            if cP == tostring(cN) then

                local function x(bQ, ...)

                local function kr(bQ)
                    local bR, bS, bU, bY = tonumber(bQ.Min) or 0, tonumber(bQ.Max) or 100, tonumber(bQ.Default), tonumber(bQ.Increment or bQ.Step)

                    local function Rr(bZ)
                        bZ = tonumber(bZ)

                        if not (bZ) then
                            return 0
                        end

                        for b_ = 0, 6, 1 do
                            local b2 = 10 ^ b_

                            if math.abs(bZ * b2 - math.round(bZ * b2)) < 1E-6 then
                                return b_
                            end
                        end

                        return 6
                    end

                    local bZ = bQ.AllowDecimals

                    bZ = (bZ == nil and {
                        (bQ.Decimals),
                    } or {bZ})[1]

                    local b_, b2, ca = bZ == nil

                    if b_ then
                        b2 = Rr(bY) > 0 or Rr(bR) > 0 or Rr(bS) > 0 or Rr(bU) > 0
                        ca = b2
                    else
                        b2 = bZ == true
                        ca = b2
                    end

                    local cd, ce, cf = tonumber(bQ.DecimalPlaces or bQ.Precision), 0

                    if ca then
                        ce = math.clamp(math.floor(cd or math.max(1, Rr(bY), Rr(bR), Rr(bS), (Rr(bU)))), 1, 6)
                        cf = ce
                    else
                        cf = ce
                    end

                    local function mr(cg)
                        cg = tonumber(cg) or 0

                        local ch = ca

                        if not (ch) then
                            return math.round(cg)
                        end

                        ch = 10 ^ cf

                        return math.round(cg * ch) / ch
                    end

                    local cg, ch = mr(bR), mr(bS)
                    local ck, cl, cm = ch < cg

                    if ck then
                        cl, cm = ch, cg
                    else
                        cl, cm = cg, ch
                    end

                    local function Xr(cn)
                        local co = ca

                        if co then
                            local cp = 10 ^ (-cf)
                            local ct = mr((math.abs(tonumber(cn) or cp)))

                            return math.max(cp, ct)
                        end

                        return math.max(1, math.round(math.abs(tonumber(cn) or 1)))
                    end

                    local cn = Xr(bY)

                    cd = type(bQ.ValueFormat or bQ.InputFormat) == 'function' and (bQ.ValueFormat or bQ.InputFormat)

                    local co = cd or nil

                    cd = type(bQ.ValueParse or bQ.InputParse) == 'function' and (bQ.ValueParse or bQ.InputParse)

                    local cp = cd or nil

                    local function hr(ct)
                        local cu = {}

                        if type(ct) ~= 'table' then
                            return cu
                        end

                        for cv, cw in ipairs(ct)do
                            if type(cw) == 'string' or type(cw) == 'number' then
                                cv = tostring(cw)

                                table.insert(cu, {
                                    Name = cv,
                                    Suffix = cv,
                                })
                            elseif type(cw) == 'table' then
                                local cx = tostring(cw.Name or cw.Text or cw.Suffix or 'Unit')
                                local cy = cw.Suffix ~= nil and tostring(cw.Suffix) or cx

                                table.insert(cu, {
                                    Name = cx,
                                    Suffix = cy,
                                    Prefix = cw.Prefix ~= nil and tostring(cw.Prefix) or nil,
                                    Format = type(cw.Format) == 'function' and cw.Format or nil,
                                    FromBase = type(cw.FromBase) == 'function' and cw.FromBase or nil,
                                    ToBase = type(cw.ToBase) == 'function' and cw.ToBase or nil,
                                })
                            end
                        end

                        return cu
                    end

                    cd, bU = bQ.Unit or bQ.Units

                    if type(cd) == 'string' then
                        bU = cd
                        cd = {
                            Options = {bU},
                        }
                    else
                        if type(cd) == 'table' and cd.Options == nil and cd.List == nil and #cd > 0 then
                            bU = cd

                    local function dk(c9)
                        local da = ca

                        if not (da) then
                            return tostring(math.round(c9))
                        end

                        local db, dc = string.format, '%.' .. tostring(cf) .. 'f'

                        return db(dc, c9)
                    end
                    local function ok()
                        local c9 = co

                        if c9 then
                            local db, dc = pcall(co, c2)

                            if db then
                                if dc ~= nil then
                                    return tostring(dc)
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        c9 = c2

                        return dk(c9)
                    end
                    local function Nk()
                        local c9, db = ok(), (cu and {
                            (ct[cL]),
                        } or {nil})[1]
                        local dc = (db and {
                            cz or db.Format,
                        } or {nil})[1]

                        if dc then
                            local dd, de = pcall(dc, c9, db.Name, db, c2)

                            if dd then
                                local df = de ~= nil

                                if df then
                                    local dg, dh = tostring(de), cG

                                    return dg, dh
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if not (db) then
                            local dd = cG

                            if not (dd) then
                                return c9, false
                            end

                            return Br(cB.Number, c9), true
                        end

                        local dd, de = db.Prefix or cK, cG

                        if not (de) then
                            return dd .. c9 .. db.Suffix, false
                        end

                        return Br(cB.Prefix, dd) .. Br(cB.Number, c9) .. Br(cB.Suffix, db.Suffix), true
                    end
                    local function Br(c9)
                        local db = tostring(c9 or ''):gsub('<.->', '')
                        local dd, de = pcall(utf8.len, db)

                        return dd and de or #db
                    end
                    local function qr(c9)
                        local db = cu and #ct > 0
                        local dd, de, df, dg = db and cx, db and 0.125 or 0.095, db and 0.195 or 0.175, db and 7 or 5
                        local dh = math.max(0, Br(c9) - dg)

                        c9 = math.clamp(de + dh * 0.0095, de, df)
                        dh, dg = 0.62 - c9, cQ ~= ''
                        df = dg and cR or 1

                    local function Br()

                            return

                    local function Hr(c9)
                        c9 = math.clamp(tonumber(c9) or cl, cl, cm)

                        local db = c9 <= cl

                        if db then
                            local dd = cl

                            return dd
                        end

                        db = c9 >= cm

                        if db then
                            local dd = cm

                            return dd
                        end

                        local dd, de = math.floor((c9 - cl) / cn + 0.5), cl
                        local df, dg = de + dd * cn, math.clamp

                        de, db, dd = mr(df), cl, cm

                        return dg(de, db, dd)
                    end
                    local function Kr()
                        Br()
                    end
                    local function wr(c9)
                        local db = c9 == nil

                        if db then
                            return nil
                        else
                            local dd = #ct

                            if dd == 0 then
                                return nil
                            end
                        end

                        db = type(c9) == 'number'

                        if db then
                            local dd, de, df = math.clamp, math.floor(c9), #ct

                            return dd(de, 1, df)
                        end

                        local dd, de = tostring(c9), ct

                        for df, dg in ipairs(de)do
                            if dg.Name == dd then
                                return df
                            elseif dg.Suffix == dd then
                                return df
                            end
                        end

                        return nil
                    end

                    local function ek()
                        local di = c9

                        if di then
                            local dj = dd

                            if not (dj) then
                                return

                            return

                        if dp.X <= 0 then

                        local dq, ds, dt = math.max(2, math.floor(di * 0.14 + 0.5)), #ct, math.max(math.floor(dm.X + 0.5), 24)
                        local du, dv = ds * di + math.max(ds - 1, 0) * dq, df

                            end

                        ds = dj.X + dm.X - dt
                        ds = math.min(ds, dn.X + dp.X - dt - 4)
                        ds = math.max(ds, dn.X + 4)
                        dv = dj.Y + dm.Y + dq
                        dv = (dv + du > dn.Y + dp.Y - 4 and {
                            (dj.Y - dq - du),
                        } or {dv})[1]

                        dq = dd
                        dq.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(dt, du)

                        ca = dm == true

                            (math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(dn) or math.max(cf, 1)), 1, 6)),

                    local function Xr(dm)
                        dm = tostring(dm or '')

                        local dn, dp, dq = {}, #dm, false

                        for ds = 1, dp, 1 do
                            local dt = dm:sub(ds, ds)

                            ds = dt:match'%d'

                            if ds then
                                table.insert(dn, dt)
                            else
                                local du = (dt == '.' or dt == ',') and ca and not dq

                                if du then
                                    table.insert(dn, '.')

                                    dq = true
                                else
                                    local dv = dt == '-'

                                    if dv then
                                        local dx = cl

                                        if dx < 0 then
                                            if #dn == 0 then
                                                table.insert(dn, dt)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        return table.concat(dn)
                    end

                    cT:GetPropertyChangedSignal'Text':Connect(function()

                    bU.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    bU.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0)
                    bU.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.9)
                    bU.ZIndex = 6
                    bU.Parent = cl

                    bU.Name = 'Button'
                    bU.Active = true
                    bU.AutoButtonColor = false
                    bU.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    bU.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    bU.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    bU.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                    bU.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    bU.Text = ''
                    bU.ZIndex = 99
                    bU.Parent = ca

                    local cm = bQ.Default == true

                    local function mr(co, cp, ct)
                        cm = co == true
                        cd.Enabled = not cm
                        cf.Enabled = cm
                        co = UDim2.fromScale(cm and 0.72 or 0.28, 0.5)

                        if ct == false then
                            cl.Position = co
                        else
                            ai[4]:Create(cl, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Position = co}):Play()
                        end

                        co = cp ~= false

                        if co then
                            x(bQ.Callback, cm)
                        end
                    end

                    W(bU, function()
                        mr(not cm, true, true)
                    end)
                    mr(cm, false, false)

                    ca = {
                        GetValue = function()
                            local co = cm

                            return co
                        end,
                        SetValue = function(co, cp)
                            mr(co, cp, true)
                        end,
                    }

                    return bR, ca
                end
                local function Or(bQ)
                    local bR = tostring(bQ.Text or bQ.Value or bQ.Name)
                    local bU, ca, cd = Nr(bQ.Name, bR)

                    cd.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.94, 0.64)

                        else
                            cd.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.025, 0.5)
                            cd.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.94, 0.64)
                        end
                    end

                    Wr(bQ.Note or bQ.Description or '')

                            cd.Text = tostring(co)
                        end,
                        GetNote = function()

                            return co
                        end,
                        SetNote = Wr,
                    }

                    return bU, bQ
                end

                local bQ, bU, cd, cf = 0.025, 0.02, 0.94, 0.3

                local function Zr(cl)
                    if not (cl) then
                        return cd
                    end

                    local cm = cl.Position.X.Scale - cl.Size.X.Scale * cl.AnchorPoint.X

                    return math.clamp(cm - bQ - bU, cf, cd)
                end
                local function zr(cl, cm, co)
                    local cp, ct, cu = bO / bE, cl:FindFirstChildOfClass'UIAspectRatioConstraint', dr(cl, 'Main')

                    cx.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

                        cx.Text = cy

                end
                local function Ar(cl)
                    local cm, co = n(cl.Name, cl.Name, tostring(cl.ButtonText or cl.Text or 'Click'), tostring(cl.ConfirmText or 'Clicked!'))
                    local cp = dr(cm, 'Action')
                    local ct = (cl.Note ~= nil and {
                        (zr(cm, cl, cp)),
                    } or {
                        (cl.Description ~= nil and {
                            (zr(cm, cl, cp)),
                        } or {nil})[1],
                    })[1]

                    W(cp, function()
                        x(cl.Callback)
                    end)

                    return cm, {
                        Press = function()
                            if co then
                                co()
                            end

                            x(cl.Callback)
                        end,
                        GetNote = ct and ct.GetNote,
                        SetNote = ct and ct.SetNote,
                    }
                end
                local function n(cl)
                    local cm, co = P(cl.Name, cl.Name, tostring(cl.Placeholder or 'Type...'))
                    local cp = dr(cm, 'Input')

                    cp.Text = co(cl.Default)

                    local ct = cm:FindFirstChild'Main'
                    local cu, cx, cy, cz, cB, cG, cL = ct:FindFirstChild'Label', ct:FindFirstChild'Field', cm:FindFirstChildOfClass'UIAspectRatioConstraint', bO / bE, bG.WideCenterX - bG.WideWidth / 2, '', Instance.new'TextLabel'

                    cL.Name = 'Note'
                    cL.Active = false
                    cL.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
                    cL.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    cL.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    cL.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.025, 0.755)
                    cL.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.max(0.3, cB - 0.045), 0.3)
                    cL.Text = ''
                    cL.TextWrapped = false
                    cL.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    cL.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                    cL.Visible = false
                    cL.ZIndex = 5

                    _(cL)

                    cL.TextTransparency = 0.32
                    cB = cL:FindFirstChildOfClass'UIStroke'

                        cB.Thickness = 0.06
                        cB.Transparency = 0.2
                    end

                        cG = tostring(cN or '')
                        cN = cG ~= ''
                        cL.Text = cG
                        cL.Visible = cN

                        if cy then
                            if cy.Parent then
                                cy.AspectRatio = cN and bO or bE
                            end
                        end

                        cm:SetAttribute('DarkResponsiveAspect', cN and bO or bE)

                        if cN then
                            cu.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.025, 0.34)
                            cu.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.65, 0.64 * cz * bP)
                            cx.Size = UDim2.fromScale(bG.WideWidth, 0.62 * cz)
                        else
                            cu.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.025, 0.5)
                            cu.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.65, 0.64)
                            cx.Size = UDim2.fromScale(bG.WideWidth, 0.62)
                        end
                    end

                    Wr(cl.Note or cl.Description or '')

                    local function mr(cN)
                        cp.Text = co(cp.Text)

                        x(cl.Callback, cp.Text, cN)
                    end

                    cp.FocusLost:Connect(mr)

                    mr = {
                        GetValue = function()
                            return cp.Text
                        end,
                        GetNote = function()
                            local cN = cG

                            return cN
                        end,
                        SetNote = Wr,
                        SetValue = function(cN, cQ)
                            cp.Text = co(cN)

                            if cQ ~= false then
                                x(cl.Callback, cp.Text, false)
                            end
                        end,
                    }

                    return cm, mr
                end
                local function P(cl)
                    local cm, co = F(cl)
                    local cp, cx, cy = bO / bE, cm:FindFirstChildOfClass'UIAspectRatioConstraint', dr(cm, 'Main')
                    local cz, cB = dr(cy, 'Label'), dr(cy, 'Switch')

                    if cx then
                        cx.AspectRatio = bO

                    cz.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.025, 0.34)
                    cz.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.62, 0.64 * cp * bP)

                    cG.Size = UDim2.fromScale(Zr(cB), 0.3)
                    cG.Text = tostring(cl.Note or cl.Description or '')
                    cG.TextWrapped = false

                    cG.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                    cG.ZIndex = 5

                    cB.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.142, 0.75 * cp)

                    local cL = cG.Text

                    co.GetNote = function()

                        return cN
                    end
                    co.SetNote = function(cN)

                end
                local function Fr(cl, cm, co)
                    local cp = cl.Options or cl.Values or cl.List

                    assert(type(cp) == 'table', 'Dropdown features must be provided as a table')
                    assert(#cp > 0, 'Dropdown requires at least one feature')

                    local function Rr(cx)
                        local cy = {}

                        for cz, cB in ipairs(cx)do
                            cy[cz] = tostring(cB)
                        end

                        return cy
                    end

                    local cx, cy, cz, cB, cG = Rr(cp), cm == true or cl.Multi == true, tostring(cl.Note or cl.Description or ''), nil, nil

                    if co then
                        cB = {}
                        cp, cm = tostring, cl.Hint or cl.Guide or cl.Prompt or (cy and 'Tick the features you want, then press the button' or 'Pick a feature, then press the button')
                        cB.Hint = cp(cm)
                        cB.ButtonText = tostring(cl.ButtonText or cl.ActionText or 'Apply')
                        cB.OnApply = function(cL, cN)
                            x(cl.Callback, cL, cN)
                        end
                        cG = cB
                    else
                        cG = cB
                    end

                    local function zr(cL)
                        local cN = type(cL) == 'number'

                        if cN then
                            local cQ, cT, cV = math.clamp, math.floor(cL), #cx

                            return cQ(cT, 1, cV)
                        end

                        cN = cx

                        for cQ, cT in ipairs(cN)do
                            if cT == tostring(cL) then
                                return cQ
                            end
                        end

                        return nil
                    end
                    local function er(cL)
                        local cN = cy and type(cL) == 'table'

                        if cN then
                            local cQ = {}

                            for cT, cV in ipairs(cL)do
                                cT = zr(cV)

                                if cT then
                                    table.insert(cQ, cx[cT])
                                end
                            end

                            return cQ
                        end

                        return zr(cL) or 1
                    end

                    local cL = Instance.new'Frame'

                    cL.Name = cl.Name
                    cL.Active = false
                    cL.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    cL.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    cL.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    cL.ClipsDescendants = false
                    cL.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                    cL.Size = UDim2.new(bF, 0, 0, 1)
                    cL.ZIndex = 3

                    local function jr()
                        if bj then
                            return bF / bh.X
                        end

                        return bF
                    end

                    bh.OnChanged(function()
                        if cL.Parent == nil then
                            return false
                        end

                        cL.Size = UDim2.new(jr(), 0, 0, math.max(1, cL.Size.Y.Offset))
                    end)

                    local cN, cQ, cT = nil, nil, 0

                    local function Mr(cV)
                        tr()

                        cT = cT + 1

                        local cW, cX = cT, cN

                        if cX then
                            cN:Destroy()
                        end

                        local cZ, c_ = ir(cl.Name .. 'Dropdown', cl.Name, cx, er(cV), cy, cG, cz)

                        cN, cQ = cZ, c_
                        cX = cN
                        cX.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
                        cV = cN
                        cV.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0)
                        cV = cN
                        cV.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
                        cN.Parent = cL

                        local function ir()
                            local c2 = cW ~= cT

                            if c2 then
                                return
                            else
                                local c3 = cN

                                if c3 ~= cZ then
                                    return
                                elseif cZ.Parent == nil then
                                    return
                                end
                            end

                            cL.Size = UDim2.new(jr(), 0, 0, math.max(1, math.ceil(cZ.AbsoluteSize.Y)))
                        end

                        cZ:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsoluteSize':Connect(ir)

                        cX = cG and 0 or #cx

                        for c2 = 1, cX, 1 do
                            cV = dr(cZ, 'Option' .. c2)

                            W(cV, function()
                                task.defer(function()
                                    ai[5].Heartbeat:Wait()

                                    local c3 = cW ~= cT

                                    if c3 then
                                        return
                                    else
                                        local c4 = cQ

                                        if c4 ~= c_ then
                                            return
                                        elseif cZ.Parent == nil then
                                            return
                                        end
                                    end

                                    x(cl.Callback, c_.GetValue())
                                end)
                            end)
                        end

                        ir()
                        task.defer(ir)
                    end

                    Mr(cl.Default)

                    cp = {
                        GetValue = function()
                            local cV = cQ.GetValue

                            return cV()
                        end,
                        SetValue = function(cV, cW)
                            cQ.SetValue(cV)

                            cV = cW ~= false

                            if cV then
                                if not (cG) then
                                    x(cl.Callback, cQ.GetValue())
                                end
                            end
                        end,
                        SetOptions = function(cV, cW, cX)
                            assert(type(cV) == 'table' and #cV > 0, 'Dropdown SetOptions can danh sach khong rong')

                            local cZ = cQ.GetValue()

                            cx = Rr(cV)

                            Mr(cW or cZ)

                            cW = cX ~= false

                            if cW then
                                if not (cG) then
                                    x(cl.Callback, cQ.GetValue())
                                end
                            end
                        end,
                        Close = function()
                            tr()
                        end,
                        GetNote = function()
                            local cV = cz

                            return cV
                        end,
                        SetNote = function(cV)
                            cz = tostring(cV or '')
                            cV = cQ

                            if cV then
                                local cW = cQ.SetNote

                                if cW then
                                    cQ.SetNote(cz)
                                end
                            end
                        end,
                        Apply = cG and function()
                            cQ.Apply()
                        end or nil,
                        SetHint = cG and function(cV)
                            cG.Hint = tostring(cV or '')

                            cQ.SetHint(cG.Hint)
                        end or nil,
                        SetActionText = cG and function(cV)
                            cG.ButtonText = tostring(cV or cG.ButtonText)

                            cQ.SetActionText(cG.ButtonText)
                        end or nil,
                    }

                    return cL, cp
                end

                local cl, cm, co = {
                    Span = 0.895,
                    NormalHeight = 0.13,
                    GapRatio = 0.1769230769230769,
                }, {
                    Scale = 0.65,
                    Spacing = 0.15,
                    Arm = 0.22,
                }, 16

                ai[18] = (Instance.new'UIListLayout')

                local cp = {}

                ai[137] = {}

                bh.OnChanged(function(cx, cy)
