                end)

                local function er(cx, cy, cz)
                    local cG = Instance.new'TextLabel'

                    cG.Name = cx
                    cG.Active = false
                    cG.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    cG.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    cG.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    cG.LayoutOrder = cy
                    cG.Size = UDim2.new(0.97, 0, 0, 36)
                    cG.Text = cx
                    cG.TextWrapped = true
                    cG.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                    cG.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                    cG.ZIndex = 4

                    B(cG, bv)

                    local cL = Instance.new'Frame'

                    cL.Name = 'ArrowClip'
                    cL.Active = false
                    cL.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    cL.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    cL.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    cL.ClipsDescendants = true
                    cL.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.4, 0.5)
                    cL.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
                    cL.ZIndex = cG.ZIndex + 2
                    cL.Parent = cG

                    local cN = Instance.new'Frame'

                    cN.Name = 'Arrow'
                    cN.Active = false
                    cN.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    cN.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    cN.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    cN.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                    cN.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
                    cN.ZIndex = cG.ZIndex + 2
                    cN.Parent = cL
                    cy = cm.Arm
                    cx = cy / 2

                    local cQ, cT, cV = 0.56569 + cx, 0.3 + 0.35355 * cx, 0.5 + 0.35355 * cx

                    cx = 0.06499999761581421 / cm.Scale

                    local function gr(cW, cX, cZ, c_)
                        local c2, c3 = ipairs, {
                            {cT, 45},
                            {
                                1 - cT,
                                -45,
                            },
                        }

                        for c4, c5 in c2(c3)do
                            c4 = Instance.new'Frame'
                            c4.Name = 'Arm'
                            c4.Active = false
                            c4.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                            c4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                            c4.BorderSizePixel = 0
                            c4.Position = UDim2.fromScale(c5[1], cV)
                            c4.Rotation = c5[2]
                            c4.Size = UDim2.fromScale(cX, cW)
                            c4.ZIndex = c_
                            c4.Parent = cN
                            c5 = Instance.new'UIGradient'
                            c5.Color = cZ
                            c5.Rotation = 90
                            c5.Parent = c4
                        end
                    end

                    gr(cy + cx * 2, cQ + cx * 2, bz, cG.ZIndex + 2)
                    gr(cy, cQ, by, cG.ZIndex + 3)

                    local cW = 0.5 - (cT - (cQ + cy + cx * 4) * 0.35355)

                    local function J()
                        local cX, cZ = cG.AbsoluteSize.X, cG.AbsoluteSize.Y

                        if cX <= 0 then
                            return
                        elseif cZ <= 0 then
                            return
                        end

                        local c_, c2, c3 = cZ * cm.Scale, cZ * cm.Spacing, math.min(cG.TextBounds.X, cX)

                        cZ = c_ * cW

                        local c4 = (cX - c3) / 2 - c2 - cZ

                        cL.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(c_ * 1.35, c_ * 1.35)
                        cL.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.max(c4, cZ), 0.5, 0)
                        cN.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(c_, c_)
                    end
                    local function D()
                        if not (cz) then
                            cL.Visible = true

                            return
                        end

                        local cX = cz.AbsolutePosition.Y
                        local cZ, c_ = cX + cz.AbsoluteSize.Y, cL.AbsolutePosition.Y
                        local c2 = c_ + cL.AbsoluteSize.Y

                        cL.Visible = c_ >= cX and c2 <= cZ
                    end

                    cG:GetPropertyChangedSignal'TextBounds':Connect(J)
                    cG:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsoluteSize':Connect(J)
                    cG:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsolutePosition':Connect(D)
                    cL:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsolutePosition':Connect(D)
                    cL:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsoluteSize':Connect(D)

                    if cz then
                        cz:GetPropertyChangedSignal'CanvasPosition':Connect(D)
                        cz:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsolutePosition':Connect(D)
                        cz:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsoluteSize':Connect(D)
                    end

                    task.defer(J)
                    task.defer(D)

                    J = Instance.new'TextButton'
                    J.Name = 'SectionButton'
                    J.Active = true
                    J.AutoButtonColor = false
                    J.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    J.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    J.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
                    J.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    J.Text = ''
                    J.ZIndex = cG.ZIndex + 4
                    J.Parent = cG

                    return cG, cN, J
                end

                local cx, cy, cz, cG, cL, cN, cQ, cT = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}

                an.optionChangedHook = nil
                an.stateChangedHook = nil
                an.configLoadedHook = nil
                an.quickRefreshHook = nil
                cx.__index = cx
                cy.__index = cy
                cz.__index = cz
                cG.__index = cG
                cL.__index = cL
                cN.__index = cN
                cQ.__index = cQ

                local cV, cX = {}, 256

                local function Ur(cZ, c_)
                    assert(type(cZ) == 'table' and cZ._kind == c_ and not cZ._destroyed, ('API nay chi dung duoc tren %s con hoat dong'):format(c_))
                end
                local function ar(cZ, c_)
                    if type(cZ) ~= 'table' then
                        return cZ
                    end

                    c_ = c_ or {}

                    if c_[cZ] then
                        return nil
                    end

                    c_[cZ] = true

                    local c2 = {}

                    for c3, c4 in pairs(cZ)do
                        local c5 = ar(c3, c_)

                        if c5 ~= nil then
                            c2[c5] = ar(c4, c_)
                        end
                    end

                    c_[cZ] = nil

                    return c2
                end
                local function gr(cZ, c_, c2)
                    if cZ == c_ then
                        return true
                    end
                    if type(cZ) ~= type(c_) then
                        return false
                    elseif type(cZ) ~= 'table' then
                        return false
                    end

                    c2 = c2 or {}

                    if c2[cZ] == c_ then
                        return true
                    end

                    c2[cZ] = c_

                    for c3, c4 in pairs(cZ)do
                        if not (gr(c4, c_[c3], c2)) then
                            return false
                        end
                    end
                    for c3 in pairs(c_)do
                        if cZ[c3] == nil then
                            return false
                        end
                    end

                    return true
                end
                local function Mr(cZ, c_, ...)
                    if type(cZ) ~= 'function' then
                        return c_
                    end

                    local c2 = table.pack(...)
                    local c3, c4 = xpcall(function()
                        return cZ(table.unpack(c2, 1, c2.n))
                    end, function(c3)
                        return debug.traceback(tostring(c3), 2)
                    end)

                    if not (c3) then
                        return c_
                    end

                    return c4
                end
                local function fr(cZ)
                    if type(cZ) == 'table' then
                        if cZ._kind == 'State' then
                            Ur(cZ, 'State')

                            return cZ
                        end
                    end
                    if type(cZ) == 'table' then
                        if cZ._kind == 'Option' then
                            Ur(cZ, 'Option')
                            assert(cZ.State, 'This feature has no state to link')

                            return cZ.State
                        end
                    end

                    error('Provide a State or a feature with a state', 3)
                end
                local function Tr(cZ)
                    cZ._primaryOption = nil

                    local c_

                    for c2 in pairs(cZ._views)do
                        if c2._destroyed then
                        else
                            if c2.Instance ~= nil then
                                c_ = c_ or c2

                                if type(c2._userCallback) == 'function' then
                                    cZ._primaryOption = c2

                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    cZ._primaryOption = c_
                end
                local function hr(cZ)
                    for c_, c2 in pairs(cZ._views)do
                        if c_._destroyed or c_.Instance == nil then
                            cZ._views[c_] = nil
                        elseif c_._controller.SetValue then
                            local c3 = Mr(c2.ToOption, cZ.Value, ar(cZ.Value), c_, cZ)

                            c_._stateApplying = true

                            xpcall(function()
                                c_._controller.SetValue(ar(c3), false)
                            end, function(c4)
                                return debug.traceback(tostring(c4), 2)
                            end)

                            c_._stateApplying = false
                        end
                    end

                    if cZ._primaryOption then
                        if cZ._primaryOption._destroyed then
                            Tr(cZ)
                        elseif cZ._primaryOption.Instance == nil then
                            Tr(cZ)
                        end
                    end
                end
                local function Sr(cZ, c_)
                    if cZ.changed[c_] then
                        return
                    end

                    cZ.changed[c_] = true

                    table.insert(cZ.order, c_)
                end

                cT[1] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3)
                    if cZ._destroyed then
                        return false
                    end
                    if c2.steps >= cX then
                        if not (c2.warned) then
                            c2.warned = true
                        end

                        return false
                    end

                    local c4 = Mr(cZ.Normalize, c_, ar(c_), cZ)

                    if c4 == cV then
                        return false
                    end

                    for c5 in pairs(cZ._groups)do
                        if not (cT[17](c5, cZ, c4)) then
                            hr(cZ)

                            return false
                        end
                    end

                    if gr(cZ.Value, c4) then
                        hr(cZ)

                        return false
                    end

                    c2.steps = c2.steps + 1
                    cZ.Value = ar(c4)

                    Sr(c2, cZ)
                    hr(cZ)

                    for c5 in pairs(cZ._groups)do
                        cT[18](c5, cZ, c2)
                    end
                    for c5, c6 in ipairs(cZ._links)do
                        if c6.Connected then
                            c5 = Mr(c6.Transform, cZ.Value, ar(cZ.Value), ar(c6.Target.Value), cZ, c6.Target)

                            if c5 ~= cV then
                                cT[1](c6.Target, c5, c2, c3)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    return true
                end

                local function Xr(cZ)
                    local c_ = cZ._registered

                    return (c_ and {
                        (type(cZ.Callback) ~= 'function'),
                    } or {
                        (not c_),
                    })[1]
                end
                local function Sr(cZ, c_, c2, c3, c4)
                    local c5 = {
                        changed = {},
                        fireCallback = c2 ~= false,
                        order = {},
                        sourceArguments = c4,
                        sourceOption = c3,
                        steps = 0,
                        warned = false,
                    }

                    cT[1](cZ, c_, c5, c3)

                    if c5.fireCallback then
                        for c6, c7 in ipairs(c5.order)do
                            x(c7.Callback, ar(c7.Value), c7, c3)

                            c_ = c7._primaryOption

                            if Xr(c7) then
                                if c_ then
                                    if not (c_._destroyed) then
                                        if type(c_._userCallback) == 'function' then
                                            if c_ == c3 and c4 then
                                                x(c_._userCallback, table.unpack(c4, 1, c4.n))
                                            else
                                                x(c_._userCallback, c_._controller.GetValue())
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            for c8, c9 in ipairs(c7._subscribers)do
                                if c9.Connected then
                                    x(c9.Callback, ar(c7.Value), c7, c3)
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        cZ = nil

                        if (c3 and {
                            (c3._destroyed),
                        } or {
                            (not c3),
                        })[1] then
                            cZ = c3

                            for c7, c9 in ipairs(c5.order)do
                                for dd in pairs(c9._views)do
                                    if not (dd._destroyed) then
                                        if not (dd._configIgnored) then
                                            cZ = dd

                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                if cZ then
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            cZ = c3
                        end
                        if cZ and an.optionChangedHook then
                            an.optionChangedHook(cZ)
                        elseif #c5.order > 0 then
                            if an.stateChangedHook then
                                an.stateChangedHook(c5.order[1])
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    return #c5.order > 0
                end
                local function Er(cZ, c_, c2)
                    local c3 = setmetatable({
                        _kind = 'State',
                        _destroyed = false,
                        _configIgnored = c_.Save == false or c_.Config == false,
                        _groups = setmetatable({}, {
                            __mode = 'k',
                        }),
                        _links = {},
                        _registered = c2 == true,
                        _subscribers = {},
                        _views = setmetatable({}, {
                            __mode = 'k',
                        }),
                        Callback = c_.Callback,
                        Name = tostring(c_.Name or 'State'),
                        Normalize = c_.Normalize,
                        Value = ar(c_.Default),
                        Window = cZ,
                    }, cL)

                    cZ._stateObjects[c3] = true

                    return c3
                end

                cT[2] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'State')

                    return ar(cZ.Value)
                end
                cT[3] = function(cZ, c_, c2)
                    Ur(cZ, 'State')
                    Sr(cZ, c_, c2, nil)

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[4] = function(cZ, c_)
                    Ur(cZ, 'State')
                    assert(type(c_) == 'function', 'Subscribe can callback function')

                    local c2 = {
                        Callback = c_,
                        Connected = true,
                        Disconnect = function(c2)
                            c2.Connected = false
                        end,
                    }

                    table.insert(cZ._subscribers, c2)

                    return c2
                end
                cT[5] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3)
                    Ur(cZ, 'State')

                    c_ = fr(c_)

                    assert(c_.Window == cZ.Window, 'Hai State phai thuoc cung Window')

                    c3 = type(c3) == 'table' and c3 or {}

                    local c4 = {
                        Connected = true,
                        Source = cZ,
                        Target = c_,
                        Transform = type(c2) == 'function' and c2 or nil,
                        Disconnect = function(c4)
                            c4.Connected = false
                        end,
                    }

                    table.insert(cZ._links, c4)

                    if c3.Immediate == true then
                        c2 = Mr(c4.Transform, cZ.Value, ar(cZ.Value), ar(c_.Value), cZ, c_)

                        if c2 ~= cV then
                            cT[3](c_, c2, c3.FireCallback)
                        end
                    end

                    return c4
                end
                cT[6] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3)
                    local c4

                    if c2 == nil then
                        c2 = true
                        c4 = c2
                    else
                        c4 = c2
                    end

                    return cT[5](cZ, c_, function(c5)
                        if c5 == true then
                            return ar(c4)
                        end

                        return cV
                    end, c3)
                end
                cT[7] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3)
                    local c4

                    if c2 == nil then
                        c2 = false
                        c4 = c2
                    else
                        c4 = c2
                    end

                    return cT[5](cZ, c_, function(c5)
                        if c5 == false then
                            return ar(c4)
                        end

                        return cV
                    end, c3)
                end
                cT[8] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3, c4)
                    assert(type(c2) == 'function', 'LinkWhen can predicate function')

                    return cT[5](cZ, c_, function(c5, c9, dd, de)
                        if c2(c5, c9, dd, de) then
                            if type(c3) == 'function' then
                                return c3(c5, c9, dd, de)
                            end

                            return ar(c3)
                        end

                        return cV
                    end, c4)
                end
                cT[9] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3)
                    assert(type(c_) == 'table', 'LinkMany can danh sach target')

                    local c4 = {
                        Connected = true,
                        Links = {},
                    }

                    for c5, c9 in ipairs(c_)do
                        table.insert(c4.Links, cT[5](cZ, c9, c2, c3))
                    end

                    c4.Disconnect = function(c9)
                        c9.Connected = false

                        for dd, de in ipairs(c9.Links)do
                            de:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end

                    return c4
                end
                cT[10] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3)
                    assert(type(c_) == 'table', 'LinkManyWhenTrue can danh sach target')

                    local c4 = {
                        Connected = true,
                        Links = {},
                    }

                    for c9, de in ipairs(c_)do
                        table.insert(c4.Links, cT[6](cZ, de, c2, c3))
                    end

                    c4.Disconnect = function(de)
                        de.Connected = false

                        for df, dg in ipairs(de.Links)do
                            dg:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end

                    return c4
                end
                cT[11] = function(cZ, c_, c2)
                    return cT[5](cZ, c_, function(c3)
                        return not c3
                    end, c2)
                end
                cT[12] = function(cZ, c_, c2, c3, c4)
                    c_ = fr(c_)

                    return {
                        Connected = true,
                        Forward = cT[5](cZ, c_, c2, c4),
                        Backward = cT[5](c_, cZ, c3, {Immediate = false}),
                        Disconnect = function(de)
                            de.Connected = false

                            de.Forward:Disconnect()
                            de.Backward:Disconnect()
                        end,
                    }
                end
                cT[13] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'State')

                    local c_, c2 = cZ.Window, {}

                    for c3 in pairs(cZ._groups)do
                        table.insert(c2, c3)
                    end
                    for c3, c4 in ipairs(c2)do
                        if not (c4._destroyed) then
                            c4:Remove(cZ)
                        end
                    end
                    for c4, de in ipairs(cZ._links)do
                        de.Connected = false
                    end
                    for de in pairs(c_._stateObjects)do
                        for dg, dj in ipairs(de._links)do
                            if dj.Source == cZ then
                                dj.Connected = false
                            elseif dj.Target == cZ then
                                dj.Connected = false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    for de, dj in ipairs(cZ._subscribers)do
                        dj.Connected = false
                    end
                    for dj in pairs(cZ._views)do
                        dj.State = nil
                        dj._stateBinding = nil
                    end

                    cZ._views = {}
                    cZ._links = {}
                    cZ._subscribers = {}
                    cZ._groups = {}
                    cZ._destroyed = true
                    c_._stateObjects[cZ] = nil

                    if cZ._registered then
                        c_._stateByName[cZ.Name] = nil

                        for dj, dm in ipairs(c_.States)do
                            if dm == cZ then
                                table.remove(c_.States, dj)

                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                cT[14] = function(cZ)
                    local c_ = 0

                    for c2 in pairs(cZ._members)do
                        c_ = (c2.Value == true and {
                            (c_ + 1),
                        } or {c_})[1]
                    end

                    return c_
                end
                cT[15] = function(cZ, c_)
                    local c2, dj = 1 / 0

                    for dm in pairs(cZ._members)do
                        if dm ~= c_ then
                            if dm.Value == true then
                                local dn = cZ._activationOrder[dm] or 0

                                if dn < c2 then
                                    c2, dj = dn, dm
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    return dj
                end
                cT[16] = function(cZ, c_, c2, dj)
                    while true do
                        local dm, dn = cT[14](cZ), cZ.MaxActive

                        if not (dm > dn) then
                            break
                        end

                        dn = cT[15](cZ, c_) or cT[15](cZ, nil)

                        if not dn then
                            break
                        end
                        if c2 then
                            if not (cT[1](dn, false, c2, nil)) then
                                break
                            end
                        else
                            dm = dn.Value == true

                            cT[3](dn, false, dj)

                            if dm then
                                if not (dn.Value ~= true) then
                                    break
                                end
                            else
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                cT[17] = function(cZ, c_, c2)
                    if c2 ~= false then
                        return true
                    elseif c_.Value ~= true then
                        return true
                    end

                    return cT[14](cZ) - 1 >= cZ.MinActive
                end
                cT[18] = function(cZ, c_, c2)
                    if c_.Value == true then
                        cZ._nextActivationOrder = cZ._nextActivationOrder + 1
                        cZ._activationOrder[c_] = cZ._nextActivationOrder

                        cT[16](cZ, c_, c2, nil)
                    else
                        cZ._activationOrder[c_] = nil
                    end
                end
                cT[19] = function(cZ, c_)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    local c2 = fr(c_)

                    assert(c2.Window == cZ.Window, 'State and Feature must belong to the same Window')

                    if cZ._members[c2] then
                        return cZ
                    end

                    cZ._members[c2] = true
                    c2._groups[cZ] = true

                    if c2.Value == true then
                        cZ._nextActivationOrder = cZ._nextActivationOrder + 1
                        cZ._activationOrder[c2] = cZ._nextActivationOrder

                        cT[16](cZ, c2, nil, false)
                    end

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[20] = function(cZ, c_)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    local c2 = fr(c_)

                    cZ._members[c2] = nil
                    cZ._activationOrder[c2] = nil
                    c2._groups[cZ] = nil

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[21] = function(cZ, c_)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')
                    assert(type(c_) == 'table', 'AddMany requires a State/Feature list')

                    for c2, dj in ipairs(c_)do
                        cT[19](cZ, dj)
                    end

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[22] = function(cZ, c_, dj)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    local dm = fr(c_)

                    assert(cZ._members[dm], 'This State/Feature is not in the group')
                    cT[3](dm, true, dj)

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[23] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    local c_ = {}

                    for dj in pairs(cZ._members)do
                        if dj.Value == true then
                            table.insert(c_, dj)
                        end
                    end

                    table.sort(c_, function(dj, dm)
                        return (cZ._activationOrder[dj] or 0) < (cZ._activationOrder[dm] or 0)
                    end)

                    return c_
                end
                cT[24] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    return cZ.MinActive, cZ.MaxActive
                end
                cT[25] = function(cZ, c_, dj)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    local dm = tonumber(c_)

                    assert(dm, 'MaxActive phai la mot so')

                    cZ.MaxActive = math.max(1, math.floor(dm))

                    if cZ.MinActive > cZ.MaxActive then
                        cZ.MinActive = cZ.MaxActive
                    end

                    cZ.AllowNone = cZ.MinActive == 0

                    cT[16](cZ, nil, nil, dj)

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[26] = function(cZ, c_)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    local dj = tonumber(c_)

                    assert(dj, 'MinActive phai la mot so')

                    cZ.MinActive = math.clamp(math.floor(dj), 0, cZ.MaxActive)
                    cZ.AllowNone = cZ.MinActive == 0

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[27] = function(cZ, c_)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    cZ.MinActive = c_ and 0 or math.max(1, cZ.MinActive)
                    cZ.AllowNone = cZ.MinActive == 0

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[28] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'ExclusiveGroup')

                    cZ._destroyed = true

                    for c_ in pairs(cZ._members)do
                        c_._groups[cZ] = nil
                    end

                    cZ._members = {}
                    cZ._activationOrder = {}

                    if cZ.Window then
                        cZ.Window._exclusiveGroupByName[cZ.Name] = nil

                        for c_, dj in ipairs(cZ.Window.ExclusiveGroups)do
                            if dj == cZ then
                                table.remove(cZ.Window.ExclusiveGroups, c_)

                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                local function Dr(cZ, c_, dj)
                    Ur(c_, 'State')
                    assert(c_.Window == cZ.Section.Tab.Window, 'State and Feature must belong to the same Window')

                    local dm = {
                        FromOption = dj.OptionToState,
                        ToOption = dj.StateToOption,
                    }

                    cZ.State = c_
                    cZ._stateBinding = dm
                    c_._views[cZ] = dm

                    if c_._primaryOption then
                        if type(c_._primaryOption._userCallback) ~= 'function' then
                            if type(cZ._userCallback) == 'function' then
                                c_._primaryOption = cZ
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        c_._primaryOption = cZ
                    end

                    hr(c_)
                end
                local function hr(cZ, c_, dj, dm)
                    local dn = setmetatable({
                        _kind = 'Option',
                        _destroyed = false,
                        _controller = dm or {},
                        _manualVisible = true,
                        _resolvedVisible = true,
                        _visibilityDependencies = {},
                        _visibilityDependents = {},
                        Section = cZ,
                        Type = c_,
                        Name = dj.Name,
                        Instance = dj,
                    }, cG)

                    table.insert(cZ.Options, dn)

                    return dn
                end

                cT[29] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    local c_ = cZ._controller.GetValue

                    if c_ then
                        return c_()
                    end

                    return nil
                end
                cT[30] = function(cZ, c_, dj)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    local dm = cZ._controller.SetValue

                    assert(dm, ('%s khong ho tro Set()'):format(cZ.Type))

                    if cZ.State then
                        if not (cZ._stateApplying) then
                            dm(c_, false)

                            local dn = cZ._controller.GetValue()
                            local dp = Mr(cZ._stateBinding.FromOption, dn, ar(dn), cZ, cZ.State)

                            Sr(cZ.State, dp, dj, cZ)

                            if dj ~= false then
                                if not (Xr(cZ.State)) then
                                    x(cZ._userCallback, cZ._controller.GetValue())
                                end
                            end

                            return cZ
                        end
                    end

                    dm(c_, dj)

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[31] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')
                    assert(cZ.State, ('%s khong co state'):format(cZ.Type))

                    return cZ.State
                end
                cT[32] = function(cZ, c_, dj)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')
                    assert(cZ._controller.GetValue and cZ._controller.SetValue, ('%s khong ho tro State'):format(cZ.Type))

                    c_ = fr(c_)

                    assert(c_.Window == cZ.Section.Tab.Window, 'State and Feature must belong to the same Window')

                    local dm = cZ.State

                    if dm then
                        dm._views[cZ] = nil

                        Tr(dm)

                        if not (dm._registered) then
                            if next(dm._views) == nil then
                                if not (dm._destroyed) then
                                    cT[13](dm)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    Dr(cZ, c_, type(dj) == 'table' and dj or {})

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[33] = function(cZ, c_, dj)
                    return cT[32](cZ, fr(c_), dj)
                end
                cT[34] = function(cZ, c_, dj, dm)
                    return cT[5](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj, dm)
                end
                cT[35] = function(cZ, c_, dj, dm)
                    return cT[6](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj, dm)
                end
                cT[36] = function(cZ, c_, dj, dm)
                    return cT[7](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj, dm)
                end
                cT[37] = function(cZ, c_, dj, dm, dn)
                    return cT[8](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj, dm, dn)
                end
                cT[38] = function(cZ, c_, dj, dm)
                    return cT[9](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj, dm)
                end
                cT[39] = function(cZ, c_, dj, dm)
                    return cT[10](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj, dm)
                end
                cT[40] = function(cZ, c_, dj)
                    return cT[11](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj)
                end
                cT[41] = function(cZ, c_, dj, dm, dn)
                    return cT[12](cT[31](cZ), c_, dj, dm, dn)
                end
                cT[42] = function(cZ, c_)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')
                    Ur(c_, 'ExclusiveGroup')
                    cT[19](c_, cZ)

                    return cZ
                end

                local function Jr(cZ)
                    local c_ = cT[2](cZ.Source)

                    if cZ.Predicate then
                        local dj, dm = pcall(cZ.Predicate, ar(c_), cZ.Source, cZ.Child)

                        return dj and dm == true
                    end

                    return gr(c_, cZ.Expected)
                end
                local function gr(cZ, c_)
                    if cZ._manualVisible == false then
                        return false
                    end

                    for dj, dm in ipairs(cZ._visibilityDependencies or {})do
                        if dm.Connected then
                            if not (Jr(dm)) then
                                return false
                            end

                            dj = dm.SourceOption

                            if dj then
                                if dj._destroyed then
                                else
                                    local dn = c_ or {}

                                    if not (dn[dj]) then
                                        dn[dj] = true

                                        if not (gr(dj, dn)) then
                                            return false
                                        end
                                    end

                                    c_ = dn
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    return true
                end

                cT[43] = function(cZ)
                    local c_ = #(cZ._visibilityDependencies or {}) > 0 and 0.96 or 1

                    c_ = cZ._subOfParent and 0.9 or c_
                    cZ._visibilityHostWidth = c_

                    if cZ._visibilityHost then
                        if cZ._visibilityHost.Parent then
                            local dj = cZ._visibilityHost.Size

                            cZ._visibilityHost.Size = UDim2.new(c_, 0, dj.Y.Scale, dj.Y.Offset)

                            return cZ._visibilityHost
                        end
                    end

                    local dj = cZ.Instance
                    local dm = dj and dj.Parent

                    if not dj or not dm then
                        return dj
                    end

                    local dn = Instance.new'Frame'

                    dn.Name = dj.Name .. 'CollapsibleContent'
                    dn.Active = false
                    dn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    dn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    dn.ClipsDescendants = true
                    dn.LayoutOrder = dj.LayoutOrder
                    dn.Size = UDim2.new(c_, 0, 0, math.max(1, dj.AbsoluteSize.Y))
                    dn.Visible = dj.Visible
                    dn.ZIndex = dj.ZIndex
                    dn.Parent = dm
                    dj.LayoutOrder = 1
                    dj.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
                    dj.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0)
                    dj.Visible = true
                    dj.Parent = dn
                    cZ._visibilityHost = dn
                    cZ._visibilityAnimating = false
                    cZ._visibilityHostConnections = cZ._visibilityHostConnections or {}

                    local function Gr()
                        if cZ._destroyed then
                            return
                        elseif dn.Parent then
                            if not (dj.Parent) then
                                return
                            end
                        else
                            return
                        end

                        local dp = math.max(1, dj.AbsoluteSize.Y, dj.Size.Y.Offset)

                        cZ._visibilityExpandedHeight = dp

                        if not (cZ._visibilityAnimating) then
                            if cZ._resolvedVisible ~= false then
                                dn.Size = UDim2.new(cZ._visibilityHostWidth or 1, 0, 0, dp)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    table.insert(cZ._visibilityHostConnections, dj:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsoluteSize':Connect(Gr))
                    table.insert(cZ._visibilityHostConnections, dj:GetPropertyChangedSignal'Size':Connect(Gr))
                    Gr()

                    return dn
                end
                cT[44] = function(cZ, dj)
                    local dm = {}

                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    local dn, dp = cZ._resolvedVisible, gr(cZ)

                    cZ._resolvedVisible = dp

                    if dn ~= dp and not cZ._cascadingVisibility then
                        cZ._cascadingVisibility = true

                        for dq, ds in ipairs(cZ._visibilityDependents or {})do
                            dq = ds.Child

                            if ds.Connected then
                                if dq then
                                    if not (dq._destroyed) then
                                        cT[44](dq, dj)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        cZ._cascadingVisibility = false
                    end

                    local dq = cT[43](cZ)

                    if not dq then
                        return cZ
                    end
                    if cZ._preSearchVisible ~= nil then
                        cZ._preSearchVisible = dp
                        dq.Visible = cZ._searchMatched == true and dp

                        task.defer(cZ.Section._refreshHeight)

                        return cZ
                    end

                    cZ._visibilityRevision = (cZ._visibilityRevision or 0) + 1

                    local ds = cZ._visibilityRevision

                    dn = cZ._visibilityTween ~= nil

                    if cZ._visibilityTween then
                        cZ._visibilityTween:Cancel()

                        cZ._visibilityTween = nil
                    end
                    if dq.Visible == dp then
                        if not dn then
                            return cZ
                        end
                    end
                    if not dp and cZ._controller.Close then
                        cZ._controller.Close()
                    end

                    dn = cZ.Instance

                    local dt = math.max(1, cZ._visibilityExpandedHeight or 0, dn and dn.AbsoluteSize.Y or 0, dn and dn.Size.Y.Offset or 0)

                    cZ._visibilityExpandedHeight = dt
                    dn = UDim2.new(cZ._visibilityHostWidth or 1, 0, 0, dp and dt or 0)

                    if dp then
                        dq.Visible = true
                    end
                    if dj == false then
                        cZ._visibilityAnimating = false
                        dq.Size = dn
                        dq.Visible = dp

                        task.defer(cZ.Section._refreshHeight)

                        return cZ
                    end

                    cZ._visibilityAnimating = true
                    dj = TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                    dm[1] = (ai[4]:Create(dq, dj, {Size = dn}))
                    cZ._visibilityTween = dm[1]
                    dn, dj = cZ._visibilityTween, function()
                        if cZ._destroyed then
                            return
                        elseif ds ~= cZ._visibilityRevision then
                            return
                        elseif not (cZ._visibilityHost) then
                            return
                        end

                        cZ._visibilityTween = nil
                        cZ._visibilityAnimating = false
                        dq.Visible = dp

                        if dp then
                            dq.Size = UDim2.new(cZ._visibilityHostWidth or 1, 0, 0, cZ._visibilityExpandedHeight or dt)
                        end

                        cZ.Section._refreshHeight()

                        if cZ.Section._stabilizeLayout then
                            cZ.Section._stabilizeLayout()
                        end
                    end

                    dn.Completed:Connect(dj)
                    cZ._visibilityTween:Play()
                    task.defer(cZ.Section._refreshHeight)

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[45] = function(cZ, dj, dm)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    cZ._manualVisible = dj == true

                    return cT[44](cZ, dm)
                end
                cT[46] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    return (cZ._visibilityHost or cZ.Instance).Visible == true
                end
                cT[47] = function(cZ, dj, dm)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    local dn = type(dm) == 'table' and dm or {}

                    dj = (type(dj) == 'string' and {
                        (cZ.Section._optionByName[dj]),
                    } or {dj})[1]

                    local dp = fr(dj)

                    assert(dp.Window == cZ.Section.Tab.Window, 'Visibility source and Feature must belong to the same Window')

                    if dj then
                        if dj._kind == 'Option' then
                            assert(dj.Type == 'toggle' or dj.Type == 'switch', 'ShowWhen source must be a Toggle/Switch or State')
                        end
                    end

                    local dq = dn.Value

                    dq = (dq == nil and {
                        (dn.Equals),
                    } or {dq})[1]

                    local ds = {
                        Child = cZ,
                        Connected = true,
                        Expected = ar(dq == nil and true or dq),
                        Predicate = type(dn.Predicate) == 'function' and dn.Predicate or nil,
                        Source = dp,
                        SourceOption = dj and dj._kind == 'Option' and dj or nil,
                        Disconnect = function(ds)
                            if not (ds.Connected) then
                                return
                            end

                            ds.Connected = false

                            if ds.Connection then
                                ds.Connection:Disconnect()
                            end
                            if ds.Child then
                                if not (ds.Child._destroyed) then
                                    cT[44](ds.Child, false)
                                end
                            end
                        end,
                    }

                    table.insert(cZ._visibilityDependencies, ds)

                    if dj then
                        if dj._kind == 'Option' then
                            table.insert(dj._visibilityDependents, ds)
                        end
                    end

                    ds.Connection = cT[4](dp, function()
                        if ds.Connected then
                            if not (cZ._destroyed) then
                                cT[44](cZ, dn.Animated ~= false)
                            end
                        end
                    end)

                    cT[44](cZ, false)

                    return ds
                end
                cT[48] = function(cZ, dj)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    dj = (type(dj) == 'string' and {
                        (cZ.Section._optionByName[dj]),
                    } or {dj})[1]

                    Ur(dj, 'Option')
                    assert(dj.Section.Tab.Window == cZ.Section.Tab.Window, 'SubOf parent and child must belong to the same Window')
                    assert(dj ~= cZ, 'An option cannot be a sub-option of itself')

                    cZ._subOfParent = dj
                    dj._subOptions = dj._subOptions or {}

                    table.insert(dj._subOptions, cZ)

                    if not (cZ._subAspectApplied) then
                        local dm = cZ.Instance and cZ.Instance:FindFirstChildOfClass'UIAspectRatioConstraint'

                        if dm then
                            cZ._subAspectApplied = true
                            dm.AspectRatio = dm.AspectRatio * 1.16
                        end
                    end

                    cT[44](cZ, false)

                    return cZ
                end
                cT[49] = function(cZ)

                end

                cT[85] = function(cZ)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Option')

                    local dj = cZ.Section

                    cZ._destroyed = true
                    cZ._visibilityRevision = (cZ._visibilityRevision or 0) + 1

                    if cZ._visibilityTween then
                        cZ._visibilityTween:Cancel()

                        cZ._visibilityTween = nil
                    end

                    for dm, dn in ipairs(cZ._visibilityHostConnections or {})do
                        if dn.Connected then
                            dn:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end

                    cZ._visibilityHostConnections = {}

                    for dn, dp in ipairs(cZ._visibilityDependencies or {})do
                        dp.Connected = false

                        if dp.Connection then
                            dp.Connection:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end

                    cZ._visibilityDependencies = {}

                    for dp, dq in ipairs(cZ._visibilityDependents or {})do
                        dq:Disconnect()
                    end

                    cZ._visibilityDependents = {}

                    if cZ._controller.Close then
                        cZ._controller.Close()
                    end
                    if cZ._controller.Destroy then
                        pcall(cZ._controller.Destroy)
                    end

                    local dq = cZ.State

                    if dq then
                        dq._views[cZ] = nil

                        Tr(dq)

                        cZ.State = nil
                        cZ._stateBinding = nil

                        if not (dq._registered) then
                            if next(dq._views) == nil then
                                if not (dq._destroyed) then
                                    cT[13](dq)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    dj._optionByName[cZ.Name] = nil

                    for ds, dt in ipairs(dj.Options)do
                        if dt == cZ then
                            table.remove(dj.Options, ds)

                            break
                        end
                    end

                    if cZ._visibilityHost then
                        cZ._visibilityHost:Destroy()

                        cZ._visibilityHost = nil
                        cZ.Instance = nil
                    elseif cZ.Instance then
                        cZ.Instance:Destroy()

                        cZ.Instance = nil
                    end

                    cZ._controller = {}

                    if not (dj._destroyed) then
                        task.defer(dj._refreshHeight)
                    end
                end

                local function gr(cZ, dj)
                    local dq = ao[1]

                    assert(type(dq) == 'function', 'Protected UI engine is not ready.')

                    return dq(cZ, dj)
                end
                local function Tr(cZ, dj, dq, ds)
                    Ur(cZ, 'Section')

                    cZ._nextOptionOrder = cZ._nextOptionOrder + 1
                    dq.LayoutOrder = cZ._nextOptionOrder
                    dq.Parent = cZ._content

                    local dt, du = gr(cZ, dq), dq:GetPropertyChangedSignal'AbsoluteSize':Connect(cZ._refreshHeight)

                    ds = ds or {}

                    local dv, dx = ds.Destroy, false

                    ds.Destroy = function()
                        local dy = dx

                        if dy then
                            return
                        end

                        dx = true

                        if dv then
                            dv()
                        end

                        dt()

                        if du.Connected then
                            du:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end

                    task.defer(cZ._refreshHeight)

                    if cZ._stabilizeLayout then
                        cZ._stabilizeLayout()
                    end

                    return hr(cZ, dj, dq, ds)
                end

                local cZ, dj = {
                    [1] = function(cZ)
                        return Ar(cZ)
                    end,
                    [2] = function(cZ)
                        return Fr(cZ, false)
                    end,
                    [3] = function(cZ)
                        return Fr(cZ, true)
                    end,
                    [4] = function(cZ)
                        return Fr(cZ, false, true)
                    end,
                    [5] = function(cZ)

                    [9] = function(cZ)
                        if cZ.Note ~= nil then
                            return P(cZ)
                        elseif cZ.Description ~= nil then
                            return P(cZ)
                        end

                        return F(cZ)
                    end,
                    [10] = (function()
                        local cZ, dj, dq, ds, dt, du, dv, dx, dy, dz = {
                            TextScale = 1,
                            TitleScale = 1,
                            Font = 'Gotham',
                            LineHeight = 1.16,
                            Padding = 1,
                            Alignment = 'Left',
                            BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
                            TextColor = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235),
                            TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7,
                            GroupColor = Color3.fromRGB(58, 255, 55),
                            GroupSpacing = true,
                            MaxLines = 0,
                            ImageLines = 2.3,
                            ItemSpacing = 0.35,
                            SpotlightLines = 5,
                            SpotlightBackgroundColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                            SpotlightBackgroundTransparency = 1,
                            SpotlightModelScale = 1,
                            SpotlightDividerColor = false,
                            ScrollBarColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                            ItemBackgroundColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                            ItemBackgroundTransparency = 1,
                            SelectedBackgroundColor = Color3.fromRGB(58, 255, 55),
                            SelectedBackgroundTransparency = 0.86,
                            HoverBackgroundColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                            HoverBackgroundTransparency = 0.93,
                            TitlePartSpacing = 0.34,

                                return dA
                            end
                            if typeof(dA) == 'EnumItem' then
                                local dB, dC = pcall(Font.fromEnum, dA)

                                if dB then
                                    return dC
                                end
                            end

                        end
                        local function Vr(dA, dB)

                                return
                            end

                            for dC, dD in pairs(dB)do
                                if cZ[dC] ~= nil then

                        local function sr(dA, dB)
                            if typeof(dA) == 'Color3' then
                                return '#' .. dA:ToHex()
                            end
                            if type(dA) == 'string' then
                                if dA ~= '' then
                                    return dA
                                end
                            end

                            return dB
                        end
                        local function rr(dA, dB)
                            if typeof(dA) == 'Color3' then
                                return dA
                            end

                                end
                            end

                        local function Br(dA)
                            return (string.gsub(tostring(dA), '[<>&]', {
                                ['<'] = '&lt;',
                                ['>'] = '&gt;',
                                ['&'] = '&amp;',
                            }))
                        end
                        local function _r(dA)
                            local dB = string.gsub(tostring(dA), '<[^>]*>', '')

                            dB = string.gsub(dB, '&lt;', '<')
                            dB = string.gsub(dB, '&gt;', '>')
                            dB = string.gsub(dB, '&amp;', '&')

                            return string.lower(dB)
                        end
                        local function qr(dA)
                            local dB = {}

                            for dC in string.gmatch(tostring(dA) .. '\n', '(.-)\n')do
                                table.insert(dB, {Text = dC})

                            if type(dA) == 'table' then

                        local function cr(dA)

                        local function Kr(dA)
                            if typeof(dA) ~= 'Instance' then
                                return nil
                            end

                            local dB, dF = pcall(function()
                                return dA:Clone()
                            end)

                            if dB then
                                if not (dF) then
                                    return nil
                                end
                            else
                                return nil
                            end

                                if dH:IsA'LuaSourceContainer' or dH:IsA'ParticleEmitter' or dH:IsA'Trail' or dH:IsA'Beam' then
                                    dH:Destroy()
                                elseif dH:IsA'Light' then
                                    dH:Destroy()
                                elseif dH:IsA'Sound' then
                                    dH:Destroy()
                                elseif dH:IsA'Fire' then
                                    dH:Destroy()
                                elseif dH:IsA'Smoke' then
                                    dH:Destroy()
                                elseif dH:IsA'Sparkles' then
                                    dH:Destroy()
                                elseif dH:IsA'BasePart' then
                                    dH.Anchored = true
                                    dH.CanCollide = false
                                    dH.CastShadow = false
                                end
                            end

                            if dF:IsA'BasePart' then
                                dF.Anchored = true
                            end

                            return dF
                        end
                        local function wr(dA)
                            local dB = game

                            if not (dA:IsDescendantOf(dB)) then
                                return false
                            end

                            dB = dA

                            while dB do
                                if dB:IsA'GuiObject' then
                                    if not (dB.Visible) then
                                        return false
                                    end
                                end
                                if dB:IsA'LayerCollector' then
                                    return dB.Enabled
                                end

                                dB = dB.Parent
                            end

                            return false
                        end

                            local function Dk(d9, ea, eb, ec, ed, ee)
                                local ef = cr(eb)

                                if #ef == 0 then
                                    if ec ~= nil then
                                        ef[1] = {
                                            Text = tostring(ec),
                                            Gradient = ed,
                                        }
                                    end
                                end

                                ec = false

                                for eg, eh in ipairs(ef)do
                                    ed = d9[eg]

                                    if ed then
                                    else
                                        ed = Ek(ea, ee)
                                        d9[eg] = ed
                                        ec = true
                                    end
                                    if ed.Label.Text ~= eh.Text then
                                        ed.Label.Text = eh.Text
                                        ec = true
                                    end
                                    if ed.GradientSource ~= eh.Gradient then
                                        ed.GradientSource = eh.Gradient

                                        xr(ed.Label, eh.Gradient)

                                        ec = true
                                    end
                                    if ed.StrokeGradientSource ~= eh.StrokeGradient then
                                        ed.StrokeGradientSource = eh.StrokeGradient

                                        xr(ed.Stroke, eh.StrokeGradient)

                                        ec = true
                                    end

                                    ec = ed.Spec ~= eh and true or ec
                                    ed.Spec = eh
                                    ed.Label.Visible = true
                                end

                                for eg = #ef + 1, #d9, 1 do
                                    eb = d9[eg]

                                    if eb.Label.Visible then
                                        eb.Label.Visible = false
                                        ec = true
                                    end

                                    eb.Spec = nil
                                end

                                return #ef > 0, ec
                            end
                            local function xr(
                                d9,
                                ea,
                                eb,
                                ec,
                                ed,
                                ee,
                                ef,
                                eg,
                                eh
                            )
                                local ei, ej = {}, 0

                                for el, em in ipairs(d9)do
                                    el = em.Spec

                                    if el then
                                        local en, eo = em.Label, math.max(8, math.floor(ee * math.max(0.3, tonumber(el.Scale) or 1) + 0.5))

                                        en.FontFace = pr(el.Font or ef)
                                        en.TextSize = eo
                                        en.TextColor3 = rr(el.Color, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
                                        en.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, ec), math.max(1, ed))

                                        local ep = em.Stroke

                                        ep.Color = rr(el.StrokeColor, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
                                        ep.Transparency = math.clamp(tonumber(el.StrokeTransparency) or 0, 0, 1)
                                        ep.Thickness = math.max(0.5, tonumber(el.StrokeThickness) or math.max(1, eo * 0.1))
                                        ep = en.TextBounds.X

                                        local eq = ep <= 0

                                        if eq then
                                            ep, d3 = (utf8.len(en.Text) or #en.Text) * eo * 0.62, true
                                        end

                                        eq = math.max(1, math.ceil(ep + eo * 0.08))

                                        table.insert(ei, {
                                            Part = em,
                                            Width = eq,
                                        })

                                        ej = ej + eq
                                    end
                                end

                                if #ei == 0 then
                                    return
                                end

                                ef = math.max(0, eh) * (#ei - 1)
                                d9, ee = ej + ef > ec and math.max(0.2, (ec - ef) / math.max(1, ej)) or 1, ef

                                for el, em in ipairs(ei)do
                                    em.Width = math.max(1, math.floor(em.Width * d9))
                                    ee = ee + em.Width
                                end

                                ef = (eg == Enum.TextXAlignment.Center and {
                                    (ea + math.max(0, (ec - ee) / 2)),
                                } or {
                                    (eg == Enum.TextXAlignment.Right and {
                                        (ea + math.max(0, ec - ee)),
                                    } or {ea})[1],
                                })[1]

                                for em, en in ipairs(ei)do
                                    ea = en.Part.Label
                                    ea.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ef, eb)
                                    ea.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(en.Width, ed)
                                    ef = ef + (en.Width + eh)
                                end
                            end

                                local dI = dx._metrics

                                Cr(dx, dA, dz)

                                if typeof(dz.Size) ~= 'UDim2' then
                                    if dz.Height == nil then
                                        if dz.HeightScale == nil then
                                            dA.Size = UDim2.new(dA.Size.X.Scale, dA.Size.X.Offset, 0, math.max(2, math.floor(dI.Unit * 0.32)))
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                dA.BackgroundColor3 = Ar(dz.Background, Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 55))
                                dF.BackgroundColor3 = Ar(dz.FillColor, Color3.fromRGB(58, 255, 55))
                                dF.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(Vr(dz.Alpha, 0), 0, 1), 1)

                                sr(dA, Vr(dz.Corner, 0.16) * dI.Unit)
                                sr(dF, Vr(dz.Corner, 0.16) * dI.Unit)
                                pr(dF, dz.FillGradient, dz.GradientRotation)
                            end)

                            dI.SetAlpha = function(dJ)
                                dz.Alpha = dJ
                                dF.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(Vr(dJ, 0), 0, 1), 1)

                                return dI
                            end

                            return dI
                        end
                        local function pr(dx, dz)
                            local dA = Instance.new'ViewportFrame'

                            dA.Name = tostring(dz.Name or 'Model')
                            dA.Ambient = Ar(dz.Ambient, Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 180))
                            dA.BackgroundColor3 = Ar(dz.Background, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
                            dA.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(Vr(dz.BackgroundTransparency, 1), 0, 1)
                            dA.BorderSizePixel = 0
                            dA.LightColor = Ar(dz.LightColor, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
                            dA.LightDirection = typeof(dz.LightDirection) == 'Vector3' and dz.LightDirection or Vector3.new(
-0.6, -1, -0.4)
                            dA.ZIndex = tonumber(dz.ZIndex) or 7

                            Hr(dx, dA, cr(dx, dz.Parent))

                            local dF = Instance.new'Camera'

                            dF.FieldOfView = Vr(dz.FieldOfView, 40)
                            dF.Parent = dA
                            dA.CurrentCamera = dF

                            local dI = {
                                Template = nil,
                                Model = nil,
                                Center = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
                                Radius = nil,
                                Distance = 10,
                                Angle = 0,
                                Spin = dz.Spin ~= false,
                                Height = Vr(dz.CameraHeight, 0.35),
                                Zoom = Vr(dz.Zoom, 1.05),
                            }

                            local function Fk()
                                if not (dI.Model) then
                                    return
                                end

                                local dJ = Vector3.new(math.sin(dI.Angle), dI.Height, math.cos(dI.Angle)).Unit

                                dF.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(dI.Center + dJ * dI.Distance, dI.Center)
                            end
                            local function Ok(dJ)
                                if dI.Model then
                                    dI.Model:Destroy()

                                    dI.Model = nil
                                end

                                dI.Template = dJ

                                local dK = Br(dJ)

                                if not (dK) then
                                    return
                                end

                                local dL, dM

                                if dK:IsA'Model' then
                                    dL, dM = dK:GetBoundingBox()
                                else
                                    if dK:IsA'BasePart' then
                                        dL, dM = dK.CFrame, dK.Size
                                    end
                                end
                                if not (dL) then
                                    dK:Destroy()

                                    return
                                end

                                dI.Center = dL.Position
                                dI.Radius = dM.Magnitude * 0.5
                                dI.Distance = dI.Radius / math.tan(math.rad(dF.FieldOfView / 2)) * dI.Zoom
                                dK.Parent = dA
                                dI.Model = dK

                                Fk()
                            end

                            local dJ = Kr(dx, dA, 'Model', dz, function()
                                local dJ = dx._metrics

                                Cr(dx, dA, dz)

                                if typeof(dz.Size) ~= 'UDim2' then
                                    if dz.Width == nil then
                                        if dz.WidthScale == nil then
                                            local dK = dA.Size.Y.Offset

                                            dA.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(dK, dK)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                if dz.Corner ~= nil then
                                    sr(dA, Vr(dz.Corner, 0) * dJ.Unit)
                                end
                                if dz.StrokeColor ~= nil or dz.StrokeThickness ~= nil then
                                    rr(dA, Ar(dz.StrokeColor, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), Vr(dz.StrokeThickness, 0.08) * dJ.Unit, dz.StrokeTransparency, Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border)
                                end

                                dJ = Vr(dz.Zoom, 1.05)

                                if dJ ~= dI.Zoom then
                                    if dI.Radius then
                                        dI.Distance = dI.Radius / math.tan(math.rad(dF.FieldOfView / 2)) * dJ
                                    end
                                end

                                dI.Zoom = dJ
                                dI.Height = Vr(dz.CameraHeight, 0.35)

                                if dz.Model ~= dI.Template then
                                    Ok(dz.Model)
                                end

                                dA.Visible = dz.Visible ~= false and dI.Model ~= nil
                            end)

                            dJ.SetModel = function(dK)
                                dz.Model = dK

                                dx._queue(dJ)

                                return dJ
                            end
                            dJ.SetSpin = function(dK)
                                dI.Spin = dK ~= false

                                return dJ
                            end
                            dJ.Step = function(dK)
                                if dI.Model then
                                    if dI.Spin then
                                        dI.Angle = (dI.Angle + dt * dK) % 6.283185307179586

                                        Fk()
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            table.insert(dx._models, dJ)

                            return dJ
                        end

                cT[86] = function(dq, ds, dv)
                    Ur(dq, 'Section')

                    local dx, dz = an[101](ds)

                cT[90] = function(dq, ds)

                            dW = false

                        local d1, d3, d4 = math.max(0, math.ceil(dU.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)), 0, 0

                        } or {d4})[1]

                    end

                    local function Or()
                        dV._measureExpandedHeight()

                            if not (ec) then

                                    dV._stabilizeLayout()
                                end
                            end
